```python
"""
Optimizes a nonnegative sequence minimizing the maximum coefficient of
np.convolve(a, a) under sum-normalization.

Main ingredients:
- Reweighted projected-gradient "Puma" search for the L-infinity convolution peak.
- Exact gradient accumulator via FFT for weighted convolution coefficients.
- LP-Frank-Wolfe direction search with sparse active-set constraints.
- Spectral flat-factor initialization / escaping candidates.
- Cheap local 2-coordinate line search for final polish.
- Multi-length refinement by interpolation and re-optimization.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    import cvxpy as cp
except Exception:
    cp = None

_MODULE_SOLVER = None


def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def norm_sum1(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = arr.sum()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return arr
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def simplex_project(v):
    n = len(v)
    if n == 0:
        return v
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    cond = u * np.arange(1, n + 1) > (cssv - 1)
    rho = np.nonzero(cond)[0]
    if len(rho) == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    rho = rho[-1]
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1.0) / float(rho + 1.0)
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)


def conv_square(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    L = next_pow2(size)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:size].astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c


def make_mask_idx(n, mtype="full"):
    n = int(n)
    nb = 2 * n - 1
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=int)
    if mtype is None or mtype == "full":
        return np.arange(nb, dtype=int)
    if mtype == "no_edges":
        if nb <= 2:
            return np.arange(nb, dtype=int)
        return np.arange(1, nb - 1, dtype=int)
    if mtype == "middle":
        start = max(0, n // 2)
        end = min(nb, 3 * n // 2)
        if end <= start:
            return np.arange(nb, dtype=int)
        return np.arange(start, end, dtype=int)
    if mtype == "center":
        start = max(0, n - 2)
        end = min(nb, n + 2)
        if end <= start:
            return np.arange(nb, dtype=int)
        return np.arange(start, end, dtype=int)
    return np.arange(nb, dtype=int)


def masked_max_idx(b, idx):
    if idx.size == 0:
        return float(np.max(b)) if b.size > 0 else 0.0
    return float(np.max(b[idx]))


def weights_b_idx(b, idx, temp=0.1, bg=0.0):
    size = b.size
    p = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
    if size == 0:
        return p
    if idx.size == 0:
        p.fill(1.0 / float(size))
        return p

    vals = b[idx]
    m = float(np.max(vals))
    if m <= 1e-300:
        p[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
        return p

    scale = max(float(temp) * m, 1e-300)
    logw = np.full(size, -1000.0, dtype=np.float64)
    diff = (vals - m) / scale
    diff = np.clip(diff, -100.0, 0.0)
    logw[idx] = diff
    w = np.exp(logw)
    wsum = float(w.sum())
    if wsum <= 1e-300:
        p[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
        return p

    p = w / wsum
    bg = float(bg)
    if bg > 0.0:
        p = (1.0 - bg) * p
        p[idx] = p[idx] + bg / float(idx.size)
    return p


def grad_weighted(a, p):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k p_k * b_k where b = conv(a,a).
    For coefficient k, db_k/d a_i = 2 a_{k-i} when valid.
    Aggregated as 2 * h, where h_i = sum_j a_j p_{i+j}.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or p.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    L = next_pow2(n + p.size - 1)
    p_rev = np.ascontiguousarray(p[::-1])
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p_rev, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * P, L)
    h = c[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    g = 2.0 * h
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    return g


def safe_eval(a):
    try:
        arr = norm_sum1(a)
        if arr.size == 0:
            return float("inf")
        out = [float(x) for x in arr]
        val = evaluate_sequence(out)
        return float(val)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def try_update(best_seq, best_score, a, idx, label):
    if a is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    arr = norm_sum1(a)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return best_seq, best_score

    b = conv_square(arr)
    if idx.size != 2 * arr.size - 1:
        idx = make_mask_idx(arr.size, "full")
    m = masked_max_idx(b, idx)
    est = 2.0 * float(arr.size) * m
    score = safe_eval(arr)

    if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score + 1e-15:
        best_seq = arr.copy()
        best_score = score
        print("NEW BEST " + label + ": score=" + repr(score) +
              " est=" + repr(est) + " n=" + str(arr.size))
    else:
        print("tried " + label + ": est=" + repr(est) +
              " score=" + repr(score) + " best=" + repr(best_score))

    sym = norm_sum1(0.5 * (arr + arr[::-1]))
    if not np.allclose(sym, arr, rtol=1e-14, atol=1e-15):
        b2 = conv_square(sym)
        m2 = masked_max_idx(b2, idx)
        est2 = 2.0 * float(sym.size) * m2
        if est2 < est - 1e-10 * max(1.0, est):
            score2 = safe_eval(sym)
            if np.isfinite(score2) and score2 < best_score + 1e-15:
                best_seq = sym.copy()
                best_score = score2
                print("NEW BEST sym " + label + ": score=" + repr(score2) +
                      " n=" + str(sym.size))
    return best_seq, best_score


def choose_mask_type(a, score):
    try:
        arr = norm_sum1(a)
        n = arr.size
        if n < 3:
            return "full"
        b = conv_square(arr)
        nb = 2 * n - 1
        if not np.isfinite(score) or score <= 0:
            return "full"
        M = float(score) / (2.0 * float(n))

        c_full = float(np.max(b))
        c_no = float(np.max(b[1:nb - 1])) if nb > 2 else c_full
        start = max(0, n // 2)
        end = min(nb, 3 * n // 2)
        c_mid = float(np.max(b[start:end])) if end > start else c_full

        best = "full"
        bestdiff = 1e30
        for name, c in (("full", c_full), ("no_edges", c_no), ("middle", c_mid)):
            if c > 0:
                diff = abs(c - M) / max(abs(M), 1e-300)
                if diff < bestdiff:
                    bestdiff = diff
                    best = name
        if bestdiff < 0.05:
            return best
        return "full"
    except Exception:
        return "full"


def central_binomial_candidate(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(1, n):
        a[k] = a[k - 1] * ((2.0 * k - 1.0) / (2.0 * k))
    return norm_sum1(a)


def deterministic_candidates(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return []
    out = []
    out.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))

    cb = central_binomial_candidate(n)
    out.append(cb)
    out.append(norm_sum1(0.5 * (cb + cb[::-1])))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out.append(norm_sum1(0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(np.pi * x)))
    out.append(norm_sum1(0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * x)))
    out.append(norm_sum1(0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)))
    out.append(norm_sum1(1.0 - 0.8 * (np.sin(np.pi * x) ** 2)))
    return out


def cyclic_shift_search(a0, idx, max_trials=200, time_limit=30.0):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return a, masked_max_idx(conv_square(a), idx)

    b = conv_square(a)
    m = masked_max_idx(b, idx)
    best = a.copy()

    if n <= 2000:
        shifts = list(range(1, n))
    else:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
        if n - 1 <= int(max_trials):
            shifts = list(range(1, n))
        else:
            shifts = [int(s) for s in rng.choice(np.arange(1, n), size=int(max_trials), replace=False)]

    for s in shifts:
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > float(time_limit):
            break
        a_s = np.roll(a, -int(s))
        m_s = masked_max_idx(conv_square(a_s), idx)
        if m_s < m - 1e-15:
            m = m_s
            best = a_s.copy()
    return best, m


def spectral_flat_iteration(start, idx, n_iters=200, gamma=0.2, time_limit=60.0):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = norm_sum1(start)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, 0.0

    size = 2 * n - 1
    L = next_pow2(size)
    bt = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if idx.size > 0:
        bt[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
    else:
        bt[:size] = 1.0 / float(size)
    T = np.fft.fft(bt, L)

    m = masked_max_idx(conv_square(a), idx)
    best = a.copy()
    best_m = m

    A = np.fft.fft(np.pad(a, (0, L - n)), L)
    mirror_idx = (-np.arange(L)) % L

    gamma = float(gamma)
    for _ in range(int(n_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > float(time_limit):
            break

        C = A * A
        mag_C = np.maximum(np.abs(C), 1e-300)
        mag_T = np.maximum(np.abs(T), 1e-300)

        log_new = (1.0 - gamma) * np.log(mag_C) + gamma * np.log(mag_T)
        log_new = np.clip(log_new, -700.0, 700.0)
        mag_new = np.exp(log_new)

        an_C = np.angle(C)
        an_T = np.angle(T)
        diff = an_T - an_C
        diff = np.where(diff > np.pi, diff - 2.0 * np.pi, diff)
        diff = np.where(diff < -np.pi, diff + 2.0 * np.pi, diff)
        an_new = an_C + gamma * diff

        C_new = mag_new * np.exp(1j * an_new)
        C_new = 0.5 * (C_new + np.conj(C_new[mirror_idx]))
        A_new = np.sqrt(C_new)
        A_new = 0.5 * (A_new + np.conj(A_new[mirror_idx]))

        a_new = np.fft.ifft(A_new).real[:n]
        a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
        s = a_new.sum()
        if s < 1e-15:
            break
        a_new = a_new / s

        a_next = 0.5 * a + 0.5 * a_new
        a_next = np.maximum(a_next, 0.0)
        s2 = a_next.sum()
        if s2 < 1e-15:
            break
        a_next = a_next / s2

        m_new = masked_max_idx(conv_square(a_next), idx)
        if m_new < best_m - 1e-15:
            best_m = m_new
            best = a_next.copy()

        if m_new <= m + 1e-12:
            a = a_next
        else:
            a = a + 0.1 * (a_next - a)
            a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
            s3 = a.sum()
            if s3 > 1e-15:
                a = a / s3
        m = m_new

    return best, best_m


def puma_search(start, time_limit, idx, temp_start=0.20, temp_end=0.03,
                eta_start=0.45, bg_start=0.04, restart_every=5000, verbose=True):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    x = norm_sum1(start)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0 or time_limit <= 0:
        return x, 0.0

    x_prev = x.copy()
    b = conv_square(x)
    m = masked_max_idx(b, idx)

    best_m = m
    best_x = x.copy()
    best_b = b.copy()

    eta = float(eta_start)
    t = 1.0
    it = 0
    stall = 0
    fails = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(777)
    step_cap = 0.08
    last_print = t0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < float(time_limit):
        frac = (time.monotonic() - t0) / max(float(time_limit), 1e-9)
        if frac > 1.0:
            frac = 1.0

        temp = float(temp_start) + (float(temp_end) - float(temp_start)) * frac
        bg = float(bg_start) * (1.0 - 0.8 * frac)

        if it > 1:
            t_new = (1.0 + np.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t * t)) / 2.0
            theta = (t - 1.0) / t_new
            if theta > 0.9:
                theta = 0.9
            if fails > 0:
                theta *= 0.5
            y = x + theta * (x - x_prev)
            t = float(t_new)
        else:
            y = x.copy()
            t = 1.0

        y = simplex_project(y)
        us = y.sum()
        if us <= 0:
            y = x.copy()
        else:
            y = y / us

        p = weights_b_idx(b, idx, temp, bg)
        g = grad_weighted(y, p)
        gmax = float(np.max(g))
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax < 1e-300:
            break

        old_m = m
        accepted = False

        for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
            e = eta * factor
            if gmax > 0.0:
                e = min(e, step_cap / gmax)
            if e <= 1e-15:
                break

            z = y - e * g
            z = simplex_project(z)
            zs = z.sum()
            if zs <= 0:
                continue
            z = z / zs

            b_z = conv_square(z)
            m_z = masked_max_idx(b_z, idx)

            if m_z < old_m - 1e-14:
                accepted = True
                x_prev = x
                x = z
                b = b_z
                m = m_z

                if m < best_m - 1e-14:
                    best_m = m
                    best_x = x.copy()
                    best_b = b.copy()
                    stall = 0
                else:
                    stall += 1

                if old_m - m_z > 1e-7 * max(1e-300, best_m):
                    eta = min(1.2, eta * 1.03)
                else:
                    eta = max(0.05, eta * 0.98)
                fails = 0
                break

        if not accepted:
            eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.75)
            x_prev = x.copy()
            t = 1.0
            fails += 1
            stall += 1

        it += 1

        if stall >= int(restart_every):
            noise = 0.02 + 0.01 * (stall // int(restart_every))
            pert = rng.normal(0.0, noise, n) * best_x
            x_r = best_x + pert + rng.normal(0.0, 1e-6, n)
            x_r = np.maximum(x_r, 0.0)
            sr = x_r.sum()
            if sr > 1e-15:
                x_r = x_r / sr
                b_r = conv_square(x_r)
                m_r = masked_max_idx(b_r, idx)
                if m_r < m:
                    x = x_r
                    b = b_r
                    m = m_r
                else:
                    x = best_x.copy()
                    b = best_b.copy()
                    m = best_m
            else:
                x = best_x.copy()
                b = best_b.copy()
                m = best_m
            x_prev = x.copy()
            t = 1.0
            eta = float(eta_start)
            stall = 0
            fails = 0

        if verbose and time.monotonic() - last_print > 20.0:
            last_print = time.monotonic()
            print("Puma it=" + str(it) +
                  " t=" + repr(round(time.monotonic() - t0, 1)) + "s" +
                  " est=" + repr(2.0 * float(n) * best_m) +
                  " m=" + repr(best_m))

    return best_x, best_m


def get_solver():
    global _MODULE_SOLVER
    if _MODULE_SOLVER is not None:
        return _MODULE_SOLVER
    if cp is None:
        return None

    names = ["CLARABEL", "PDLP", "SCS", "ECOS", "ECOS_BB"]
    for name in names:
        solver = getattr(cp, name, None)
        if solver is None:
            continue
        try:
            x = cp.Variable(10, nonneg=True)
            prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cp.sum(x)), [cp.sum(x) == 1, x <= 0.5])
            prob.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)
            if prob.status in ("optimal", "optimal_inaccurate"):
                _MODULE_SOLVER = solver
                return solver
        except Exception:
            continue
    return None


def lp_fw_search(a0, time_limit, idx, solver, initial_K=120, max_K=250, verbose=True):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    x = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = x.size
    b = conv_square(x)
    m = masked_max_idx(b, idx)
    best = x.copy()
    best_m = m

    if cp is None or solver is None or idx.size == 0 or n == 0:
        return best, best_m

    cap_K = max(30, min(int(max_K), 2_000_000 // max(1, n)))
    K = min(int(initial_K), cap_K)
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = t0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < float(time_limit) and no_improve < 200:
        if idx.size < K:
            K = max(0, int(idx.size))
        if K <= 0:
            break

        sel = b[idx]
        order = np.argsort(sel)[::-1][:K]
        active = idx[order]
        if active.size == 0:
            break

        A = np.zeros((active.size, n), dtype=np.float64)
        for r in range(active.size):
            ks = int(active[r])
            s = max(0, ks - n + 1)
            e = min(n, ks + 1)
            if s < e:
                A[r, s:e] = x[ks - np.arange(s, e)]

        zvar = cp.Variable(n, nonneg=True)
        Mvar = cp.Variable(nonneg=True)
        constraints = [cp.sum(zvar) == 1.0, A @ zvar <= Mvar]
        prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(Mvar), constraints)

        try:
            prob.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)
        except Exception:
            break

        if prob.status not in ("optimal", "optimal_inaccurate"):
            break

        try:
            z = np.asarray(zvar.value, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        except Exception:
            break

        z = np.maximum(z, 0.0)
        sz = z.sum()
        if sz < 1e-12:
            break
        z = z / sz

        best_alpha = 0.0
        best_c = m
        best_trial = None
        for alpha in (1.0, 0.75, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.2):
            trial = (1.0 - alpha) * x + alpha * z
            trial = np.maximum(trial, 0.0)
            st = trial.sum()
            if st < 1e-12:
                continue
            trial = trial / st
            b_t = conv_square(trial)
            c_t = masked_max_idx(b_t, idx)
            if c_t < best_c - 1e-15:
                best_c = c_t
                best_alpha = alpha
                best_trial = trial

        if best_trial is not None and best_c < m - 1e-14:
            x = best_trial
            m = best_c
            b = conv_square(x)
            if m < best_m - 1e-14:
                best_m = m
                best = x.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                K = min(cap_K, K + 10)
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            K = max(30, K // 2)

        it += 1
        if verbose and time.monotonic() - last_print > 30.0:
            last_print = time.monotonic()
            print("LP-FW it=" + str(it) + " K=" + str(K) +
                  " est=" + repr(2.0 * float(n) * best_m))

    return best, best_m


def local_pair_search(a0, time_limit, idx):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    x = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = x.size
    b = conv_square(x)
    m = masked_max_idx(b, idx)
    best = x.copy()
    best_m = m
    if idx.size == 0 or n == 0:
        return best, best_m

    rng = np.random.default_rng(4242)
    no_improve = 0
    frac_list = (0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 0.9)

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < float(time_limit):
        sel = b[idx]
        order = np.argsort(sel)[::-1]
        top = min(30, order.size)
        active = idx[order[:top]].astype(int).tolist()
        if not active:
            break
        active_b = np.array([float(b[k]) for k in active], dtype=np.float64)

        risk = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in active:
            ks = int(k)
            s = max(0, ks - n + 1)
            e = min(n, ks + 1)
            if s < e:
                idxs = np.arange(s, e)
                risk[s:e] += x[ks - idxs]

        ord_risk = np.argsort(risk)
        sources = ord_risk[-40:]
        dests = ord_risk[:40]
        if sources.size == 0 or dests.size == 0:
            break

        improved = False
        for _ in range(80):
            if time.monotonic() - t0 > float(time_limit):
                break

            si = int(sources[rng.integers(sources.size)])
            di = int(dests[rng.integers(dests.size)])
            if si == di or x[si] <= 1e-14:
                continue

            vals_s = np.empty(len(active), dtype=np.float64)
            vals_d = np.empty(len(active), dtype=np.float64)
            quads = np.empty(len(active), dtype=np.float64)

            for j, k in enumerate(active):
                ks = int(k)
                js = ks - si
                jd = ks - di
                vals_s[j] = float(x[js]) if 0 <= js < n else 0.0
                vals_d[j] = float(x[jd]) if 0 <= jd < n else 0.0
                quads[j] = 1.0 if si + di == ks else 0.0

            ai = float(x[si])
            best_delta = 0.0
            best_sur = m

            for frac in frac_list:
                delta = frac * ai
                if delta <= 0.0:
                    continue
                c = active_b + 2.0 * delta * (vals_d - vals_s) - 2.0 * delta * delta * quads
                sur = float(np.max(c))
                if sur < best_sur - 1e-15:
                    best_sur = sur
                    best_delta = delta

            if best_delta > 0.0 and best_sur < m - 1e-13:
                x_try = x.copy()
                x_try[si] -= best_delta
                x_try[di] += best_delta
                x_try = np.maximum(x_try, 0.0)
                ss = x_try.sum()
                if ss <= 0:
                    continue
                x_try = x_try / ss

                b_try = conv_square(x_try)
                m_try = masked_max_idx(b_try, idx)
                if m_try < best_m - 1e-14:
                    x = x_try
                    b = b_try
                    m = m_try
                    best_m = m_try
                    best = x.copy()
                    improved = True
                    no_improve = 0
                else:
                    no_improve += 1

        if not improved:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 40:
                break
            noise = 1e-3 + 1e-4 * no_improve
            x = best + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n) * best
            x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
            ss = x.sum()
            if ss > 0:
                x = x / ss
                b = conv_square(x)
                m = masked_max_idx(b, idx)
                if m < best_m:
                    best_m = m
                    best = x.copy()

    return best, best_m


def interpolate_sequence(f, new_n):
    f = norm_sum1(f)
    old_n = f.size
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if new_n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if old_n == new_n:
        return f.copy()
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    g = np.interp(new_x, old_x, f)
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    sg = g.sum()
    if sg < 1e-15:
        g = np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    else:
        g = g / sg
    return g


def load_parent():
    try:
        p = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        p = None
    if p is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
        s = arr.sum()
        if arr.size > 0 and s > 1e-8:
            return arr / s
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    time_limit = min(1740.0, total_budget - 60.0)
    if time_limit < 120.0:
        time_limit = 120.0

    anchored = load_parent()
    if anchored is None or anchored.size == 0:
        anchored = central_binomial_candidate(6000)

    MAX_N = 10000
    if anchored.size > MAX_N:
        anchored = interpolate_sequence(anchored, MAX_N)
    anchored = norm_sum1(anchored)

    base_score = safe_eval(anchored)
    best_seq = anchored.copy()
    best_score = base_score

    mask_type = choose_mask_type(anchored, base_score)
    n_cur = best_seq.size
    idx_cur = make_mask_idx(n_cur, mask_type)

    print("Initial: n=" + str(n_cur) +
          " score=" + repr(best_score) +
          " mask_type=" + str(mask_type))

    # Deterministic global-ish candidates.
    rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 60.0:
        cands = deterministic_candidates(n_cur)
        scored = []
        for c in cands:
            b = conv_square(c)
            m = masked_max_idx(b, idx_cur)
            scored.append((2.0 * float(c.size) * m, c))
        scored.sort(key=lambda pair: pair[0])
        for j, (_, c) in enumerate(scored[:3]):
            best_seq, best_score = try_update(
                best_seq, best_score, c, idx_cur, "det" + str(j))
            n_cur = best_seq.size
            idx_cur = make_mask_idx(n_cur, mask_type)

    # Spectral flat-factor perturbation.
    rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 80.0:
        t_spec = min(60.0, rem * 0.05)
        a_spec, _ = spectral_flat_iteration(best_seq, idx_cur, n_iters=160, gamma=0.20, time_limit=t_spec)
        best_seq, best_score = try_update(best_seq, best_score, a_spec, idx_cur, "spectral")
        n_cur = best_seq.size
        idx_cur = make_mask_idx(n_cur, mask_type)

    # Cyclic boundary search.
    rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 30.0:
        a_shift, _ = cyclic_shift_search(best_seq, idx_cur, max_trials=200, time_limit=min(20.0, rem * 0.03))
        best_seq, best_score = try_update(best_seq, best_score, a_shift, idx_cur, "shift")
        n_cur = best_seq.size
        idx_cur = make_mask_idx(n_cur, mask_type)

    # Main Puma pass.
    rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 120.0:
        t1 = min(480.0, rem * 0.32)
        temp1 = 0.18 if np.isfinite(best_score) and best_score < 1.51 else 0.28
        a1, _ = puma_search(
            best_seq, t1, idx_cur,
            temp_start=temp1, temp_end=0.035,
            eta_start=0.45, bg_start=0.04, restart_every=5000)
        best_seq, best_score = try_update(best_seq, best_score, a1, idx_cur, "puma1")
        n_cur = best_seq.size
        idx_cur = make_mask_idx(n_cur, mask_type)

    # LP-Frank-Wolfe burst.
    rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 150.0 and n_cur <= 12000:
        solver = get_solver()
        if solver is not None:
            t2 = min(240.0, rem * 0.18)
            a2, _ = lp_fw_search(best_seq, t2, idx_cur, solver,
                                 initial_K=120, max_K=250)
            best_seq, best_score = try_update(best_shen if False else best_seq, best_score, a2, idx_cur, "lp1")
            n_cur = best_seq.size
            idx_cur = make_mask_idx(n_cur, mask_type)

    # Second Puma, lower temperature.
    rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 100.0:
        t3 = min(360.0, rem * 0.28)
        a3, _ = puma_search(
            best_seq, t3, idx_cur,
            temp_start=0.09, temp_end=0.012,
            eta_start=0.30, bg_start=0.02, restart_every=4000)
        best_seq, best_score = try_update(best_seq, best_score, a3, idx_cur, "puma2")
        n_cur = best_seq.size
        idx_cur = make_mask_idx(n_cur, mask_type)

    # Local 2-exchange polish.
    rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 60.0:
        t4 = min(100.0, rem * 0.10)
        a4, _ = local_pair_search(best_seq, t4, idx_cur)
        best_seq, best_score = try_update(best_seq, best_score, a4, idx_cur, "local1")
        n_cur = best_seq.size
        idx_cur = make_mask_idx(n_cur, mask_type)

    # Multi-length refinement.
    rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    old_n = best_seq.size
    if rem > 250.0 and old_n < 10000:
        new_n = min(10000, old_n + max(500, old_n // 4))
        idx_new = make_mask_idx(new_n, mask_type)
        a_long = interpolate_sequence(best_seq, new_n)

        t5 = min(260.0, rem * 0.30)
        a5, _ = puma_search(
            a_long, t5, idx_new,
            temp_start=0.18, temp_end=0.02,
            eta_start=0.35, bg_start=0.03, restart_every=4000)
        best_seq, best_score = try_update(best_seq, best_score, a5, idx_new, "long_puma")

        rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        n_cur = best_seq.size
        idx_cur = make_mask_idx(n_cur, mask_type)
        if rem > 120.0:
            t6 = min(180.0, rem * 0.25)
            a6, _ = puma_search(
                best_seq, t6, idx_cur,
                temp_start=0.07, temp_end=0.008,
                eta_start=0.25, bg_start=0.01, restart_every=3000)
            best_seq, best_score = try_update(best_seq, best_score, a6, idx_cur, "long_puma2")
            n_cur = best_seq.size
            idx_cur = make_mask_idx(n_cur, mask_type)

            rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
            if rem > 40.0:
                t6b = min(60.0, rem * 0.40)
                a6b, _ = local_pair_search(best_seq, t6b, idx_cur)
                best_seq, best_score = try_update(best_seq, best_score, a6b, idx_cur, "long_local")
                n_cur = best_seq.size
                idx_cur = make_mask_idx(n_cur, mask_type)

    # Final low-temperature Puma polish.
    rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 60.0:
        t7 = min(180.0, rem * 0.50)
        a7, _ = puma_search(
            best_seq, t7, idx_cur,
            temp_start=0.05, temp_end=0.004,
            eta_start=0.22, bg_start=0.0, restart_every=2500)
        best_seq, best_score = try_update(best_seq, best_score, a7, idx_cur, "final_puma")
        n_cur = best_seq.size
        idx_cur = make_mask_idx(n_cur, mask_type)

    # Final local polish.
    rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 25.0:
        t8 = min(60.0, rem * 0.60)
        a8, _ = local_pair_search(best_seq, t8, idx_cur)
        best_seq, best_score = try_update(best_seq, best_score, a8, idx_cur, "final_local")

    final = norm_sum1(best_seq)
    if final.size == 0:
        final = anchored.copy()

    final_score = safe_eval(final)
    if np.isfinite(final_score) and final_score < best_score:
        best_score = final_score

    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        final = norm_sum1(anchored)
        final_score = safe_eval(final)

    out = [float(v) for v in np.asarray(final, dtype=np.float64)]
    print("FINAL: score=" + repr(final_score) + " n=" + str(len(out)) +
          " time=" + repr(round(time.monotonic() - t0, 1)) + "s")
    return out
```
